# `spo_alt_opponents_colors.ips` — Summary

Gives all 16 opponents alternate color palettes across the three places they appear on screen: the in-fight body sprite you're punching, the small HUD/opponent-select portrait, and the large pre-fight profile portrait. The alt look is a warmer, restyled take on each fighter, with a single unified backdrop behind every large portrait in place of vanilla's four per-circuit backdrop colors.

## What it does

- **In-fight body sprite** — the opponent's on-screen sprite uses the alt palette.
- **Small portrait** — the HUD and opponent-select portrait match.
- **Large portrait** — the pre-fight profile screen uses the alt palette over a **unified backdrop** (a soft rose gradient, darker `#B8858D` / lighter `#E9BFC9`) that replaces vanilla's per-circuit backdrops (minor=blue, major=green, world=beige, special=purple).
- The alt body palette holds through per-fighter mid-fight state changes (e.g. Masked Muscle's spit attack returning to normal).
- **Bonus vanilla fix:** Bear Hugger's large-portrait outline slot, the one opponent shipped with a faint blue tint instead of pure white, is corrected to white to match everyone else.

### When the alt palettes turn on

- **Iron Circuit** — automatically on for all three contexts during an iron gauntlet run (cooperates with `spo_iron_circuit.ips`).
- **Hold SELECT (P1)** during the pre-fight load on any circuit — turns on the body + small portrait for that match.
- **Hold SELECT on the P2 controller in VERSUS mode** — same, for VS matches.

The override applies to the current match only. The large portrait follows the iron-circuit auto-trigger.

---

## Alt palettes

Slot C0 (CGRAM index 0, `$3800`) is always unchanged. ✓ marks slots that differ from vanilla.

**Small portrait:** for most fighters, A-slots mirror the body overrides for shared vanilla colors (typically A1–AA); AB–AF are unchanged (portrait background/outline). Fighters whose portrait ramp doesn't line up with the body slot order — MM, Rick, Nick — have per-fighter exceptions noted in their sections below.

---

## Special attack palettes (Pal 3)

Kept vanilla — these are projectile / effect sprite palettes, not body palette swaps.
The `#840084` sentinel entries visible below are unused slots that never reach CGRAM.

## Compatibility

- **Apply on top of**: `spo_alt_glove_colors.ips` (which is itself applied on top of the original `Super Punch-Out!! (USA).sfc` ROM, MD5 `97fe7d7d2a1017f8480e60a365a373f0`). This patch is **not** standalone against pure vanilla: its in-fight Hook 1 at `$00:97E5` rewrites the alt-glove hook (`JSL $0D:FDD2` → `JSL $40:847E`) and chains back to `$0D:FDD2`, so alt-glove must be present first. The patch expands the ROM from 2MB to 2.5MB (ExLoROM) and rewrites the internal checksum, so no separate checksum fix is needed.
- **Apply order**: `vanilla → spo_alt_glove_colors.ips → spo_alt_opponents_colors.ips`.
- **Conflicts with**: nothing in this repo. Reserves WRAM bytes `$7E:1D75` and `$7E:1D76` for its own use.
- **Cooperates with `spo_iron_circuit.ips`** via the shared WRAM byte `$7E:1D71` (iron flag) — during an iron run, all alt palettes turn on automatically. Either patch works without the other.
- **Controller buttons**: SELECT is reserved here for the alt-opponent override, while white gloves use L+R, so the two selectors don't collide.
- **SE 2.0**: bundled in. Chains to the bundle's alt-glove hook so glove colors keep working.

## Building

Built by [`scripts/build_spo_alt_opponents_colors.py`](../../scripts/build_spo_alt_opponents_colors.py):

```
python scripts/build_spo_alt_opponents_colors.py <vanilla.sfc> [out.sfc]
```

The builder applies the alt-glove prerequisite to the vanilla ROM, then applies alt-opp's own hooks, stubs, and palette tables, stamps the ExLoROM split+repeat SNES header checksum, and writes `patches/standalone/spo_alt_opponents_colors.ips`. The emitted IPS carries only alt-opp's own bytes (the shared alt-glove hook is identical on both sides of the diff), so it applies cleanly on top of `spo_alt_glove_colors.ips`. Pass an optional output path to also emit the patched `.sfc`.

## See also

- [`ALT_GLOVE_COLORS.md`](ALT_GLOVE_COLORS.md) — the player glove-color selector that shares controller-button conventions.
- [`IRON_CIRCUIT.md`](IRON_CIRCUIT.md) — owner of the iron-circuit flag `$7E:1D71` that auto-enables these palettes.
